We present AwakeForest, an interactive end-to-end platform designed for large-scale forest imagery that integrates model-assisted inference, automatic annotation, and human-in-the-loop refinement within a single workflow. Our platform supports plug-and-play integration of pretrained models and enables scalable interaction with forest imagery ranging from standard aerial scenes to large orthomosaics that can span several gigabytes to hundreds of gigabytes. AwakeForest produces analysis-ready outputs that can be directly used for downstream analysis and to support iterative model and annotation updates on new scenes. We demonstrate the system on the PALMS dataset and illustrate how AwakeForest supports an end-to-end workflow for practical forest management and analysis.

</details>


### [254] [Dense Reward for Multi-View 3D Reasoning with Global Maps and Local Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23557)
*Jiho Choi,Seonho Lee,Seojeong Park,Hyunjung Shim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DR-MV3D提出了一种基于密集奖励的多视图3D VQA学习框架，通过全局地图一致性奖励和局部轨迹奖励，结合GRPO策略优化，使多模态大模型能够在无需人工标注的情况下学习中间推理步骤，在多个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型在多视图3D VQA中仅依赖稀疏的答案级监督，导致跨视图推理不一致且视角选择脆弱，缺乏对中间推理过程的显式监督。

Method: 将MV3D-VQA分解为三个可学习的步骤：(i) 异中心全局地图构建，(ii) 问题条件化的视角轨迹规划，(iii) 自我中心接地用于答案预测。引入两种奖励：全局一致性奖励（利用冻结的3D视觉基础模型VGGT+SAM3生成几何一致的伪目标）和局部轨迹奖励（监督有序视角选择），通过GRPO进行轨迹级策略优化。

Result: 在MindCube、VSI-Bench和BLINK (MV)上，DR-MV3D一致优于强多图像基线方法。

Conclusion: 过程级的密集监督对于多视图3D推理是有效的，将中间推理步骤显式奖励化可以弥补稀疏答案监督的不足。

Abstract: Multi-view 3D Visual Question Answering (MV3D-VQA) requires integrating partial observations into a coherent 3D scene representation and selecting informative viewpoints for multi-step spatial reasoning. However, current multimodal LLMs are typically trained with sparse, answer-level supervision, which often yields inconsistent cross-view reasoning and brittle view selection. We present DR-MV3D (Dense Reward for MV3D-VQA), a map-grounded learning framework that provides dense, verifiable rewards to supervise the reasoning process. Our approach decomposes MV3D-VQA into (i) allocentric global map construction, (ii) question-conditioned view-trajectory planning, and (iii) egocentric grounding for answer prediction. To make intermediate steps learnable without manual annotations, we introduce two rewards: a global consistency reward that aligns the predicted map with geometry-consistent pseudo targets from frozen 3D vision foundation models (e.g., VGGT + SAM3), and a local trajectory reward that supervises ordered viewpoint selection. We optimize the full pipeline with trajectory-level policy optimization (GRPO). Experiments on MindCube, VSI-Bench, and BLINK (MV) show that DR-MV3D consistently improves over strong multi-image baselines, supporting the effectiveness of process-level dense supervision for multi-view 3D reasoning.

</details>


### [255] [Polycepta: Object-Centric Appearance Estimation for Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23604)
*Mohamed Nagy,Naoufel Werghi,Jorge Dias,Majid Khonji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Polycepta将外观建模重新定义为递归估计问题，为每个跟踪目标构建并持续更新独立的外观状态，使得未来外观表征可以从累积观测中估计，无需帧级匹配。该方法在KITTI、Waymo和MOT17上减少了身份切换，在RobMOT框架中以90.57Hz实时运行，在KITTI上达到92.27% MOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MOT中基于检测的跟踪依赖静态外观描述符，这些描述符帧间独立且鲁棒性有限；同时实时系统因计算成本过高而完全放弃外观线索，仅依赖运动预测和几何关联，导致身份切换问题严重。

Method: 提出Polycepta框架，将外观建模转化为递归估计问题：为每个目标构建独立外观状态，通过累积观测不断更新，并学习构建目标特定表征的构造方式而非记忆具体内容，从而实现对未见类别的外观估计。随推理过程目标状态演化，外观估计质量持续提升。

Result: 在KITTI、Waymo Open Dataset和MOT17上一致减少身份切换并提升跟踪性能；集成到RobMOT后以90.57Hz运行，在KITTI上取得92.27% MOTA的SOTA结果。

Conclusion: 将外观建模从静态匹配重新定义为递归状态估计是MOT的有效范式，能够在不牺牲实时性的前提下显著降低身份切换错误。

Abstract: The tracking-by-detection paradigm in multi-object tracking (MOT) typically relies on static appearance descriptors to complement motion estimation. However, these descriptors are frame-independent, limiting their robustness as visual cues. Since such descriptors are often obtained from computationally intensive pretrained backbones, real-time MOT systems frequently abandon appearance cues altogether and rely solely on motion prediction and geometric association. In this work, we introduce Polycepta, an object-centric appearance state estimation framework that reformulates appearance modeling as a recursive estimation problem rather than a frame-wise matching task. Polycepta constructs and continuously updates an independent appearance state for each tracked object, enabling future appearance representations to be estimated from accumulated observations. Polycepta is encouraged to learn the appearance-state construction of object-specific representations rather than memorize them through a proposed learning strategy, enabling appearance estimation for unseen classes. A key property of Polycepta is that the quality of appearance estimation improves as object states evolve during inference. While conventional appearance descriptors remain static or degrade over time, Polycepta progressively refines appearance estimates as additional observations are accumulated. Extensive experiments on KITTI, the Waymo Open Dataset, and MOT17 demonstrate consistent reductions in identity switches and improvements in tracking performance when integrated into the tracking-by-detection pipelines. Polycepta operates at 90.57 Hz and delivers state-of-the-art performance on the KITTI benchmark when integrated into the RobMOT framework, achieving a MOTA of 92.27\%.

</details>


### [256] [Vera: A Layered Diffusion Model for Content-Preserving Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23610)
*Hongkai Zheng,Ta-Ying Cheng,Benjamin Klein,Yisong Yue,Zhuoning Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Vera是一种分层扩散框架，通过生成编辑层和alpha遮罩与源视频合成，利用MoT架构实现内容保持的视频编辑，在保持源内容不变的同时进行创意编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型在编辑时会重新生成每个像素，导致本应保持不变的角色或背景场景等元素被意外修改，缺乏内容保持能力。

Method: 提出分层扩散框架Vera，将编辑任务分解为生成编辑层和alpha遮罩，并与源视频合成；将文本到视频的DiT扩展为Mixture-of-Transformers架构，通过联合自注意力实现各层间的交互；构建高质量分层训练数据集。

Result: 在定量基准和人类偏好研究中，Vera在内容保持方面优于主流开源视频编辑模型，在编辑质量方面具有竞争力，仅使用486K帧分层训练数据。

Conclusion: 通过分层设计将创意编辑与内容保持解耦，在内容保持任务中达到SOTA，验证了分层扩散在视频编辑中的有效性。

Abstract: Video diffusion models have enabled remarkable progress in video generation and editing. However, content preservation remains a core challenge: existing methods regenerate every pixel and often alter elements that should remain unchanged, such as characters or background scenes. We introduce Vera, a layered diffusion framework for content-preserving video editing. Instead of regenerating the entire video, Vera generates an edit layer along with an alpha matte for compositing with the source video, separating creative editing from content preservation by design. To encourage coherent composition with the source video, we extend the text-to-video DiT into a Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architecture, with separate DiTs for each layer that interact through joint self-attention. To support the training of Vera, we further construct a high-quality layered dataset with accurate alpha mattes, diverse scenes and dynamics, and visual effects. Across our quantitative benchmark and human preference study, Vera outperforms leading open-source video editing models in content preservation while remaining competitive in edit quality, using 486K frames of layered training data.

</details>


### [257] [Data Selection Through Iterative Self-Filtering for Vision-Language Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23611)
*Andrei Liviu Nicolicioiu,Sarvjeet Singh Ghotra,Morgane M. Moss,Aaron Courville*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种自举式的Self-Filtering方法，通过模型在训练过程中动态自筛选高质量数据来迭代训练CLIP模型，无需额外数据或预训练模型即可提升视觉语言模型的下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模视觉语言数据集存在严重噪声问题，人工审查不可行，现有方法依赖启发式规则、精心筛选的参考数据集或预训练模型，缺乏一种能够自适应的、不依赖外部资源的自动化数据筛选机制。

Method: Self-Filtering方法：在CLIP模型训练过程中，模型自身对训练样本进行评分，平衡筛选出的高置信度干净样本和来自整体分布的多样化样本，构建不断演化的数据集；模型训练与数据筛选交替迭代进行。

Result: 在多种视觉语言下游任务上，使用Self-Filtering方法筛选后的数据集训练模型，相比原始噪声数据训练取得了性能提升，且不需要额外数据或预训练模型。

Conclusion: 模型自身可以作为有效的数据筛选器，通过迭代自举的方式不断优化训练数据质量，是一种实用且可扩展的噪声数据清洗方案。

Abstract: The availability of large amounts of clean data is paramount to training neural networks. However, at large scales, manual oversight is impractical, resulting in sizeable datasets that can be very noisy. Attempts to mitigate this obstacle to producing performant vision-language models have so far involved heuristics, curated reference datasets, and using pre-trained models. Here we propose a novel, bootstrapped method in which a CLIP model is trained on an evolving, self-selected dataset. This evolving dataset constitutes a balance of filtered, highly probable clean samples as well as diverse samples from the entire distribution. Our proposed Self-Filtering method iterates between training the model and selecting a subsequently improved data mixture. Training on vision-language datasets filtered by the proposed approach improves downstream performance without the need for additional data or pre-trained models.

</details>


### [258] [Hedgementation = Hedgerow Segmentation: A Remote Sensing Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23615)
*Nathan Senyard,Salem Hamdani,Astrid Zhang,Derek Wang,Evan Shelhamer,Mathias Lécuyer,Joséphine Gantois*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Hedgementation——一个用于评估机器学习模型在国家级尺度、10m²空间分辨率下进行树篱映射的基准。融合多源遥感数据和法国树篱清单的真实标签，测试三种基线模型在空间距离和气候区上的泛化能力，涵盖监督和自监督学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树篱是农业生态系统中重要的细粒度特征，但目前缺乏在国家级大尺度下评估ML模型进行树篱映射的统一基准。

Method: 整合多源遥感数据产品和法国树篱清单的地面真值标签，构建基准。测试三种基线模型在空间距离泛化和跨气候区泛化任务上的表现，评估监督与自监督学习方法的有效性。

Result: 建立了国家级尺度的树篱映射基准，量化了基线模型在空间泛化和跨气候区泛化上的性能差异。

Conclusion: 该基准为评估遥感领域细粒度农业特征映射模型提供了标准化测试平台，代码已开源。

Abstract: We propose Hedgementation: a new benchmark to evaluate machine learning models for hedgerow mapping from remote sensing data at country scale and 10m$^2$ spatial resolution. We combine and harmonize multiple remote sensing data products and ground truth labels sourced from a hedgerow inventory in France. We measure the ability of three baseline models to generalize across spatial distance, and across climatic zones, a more explicitly challenging task. Our benchmark tests both supervised and self-supervised learning approaches for remote sensing, applied to tracking fine-scale features of high agricultural importance. The code to reproduce the benchmark and baselines results is available at https://github.com/hedgementation/hedgementation.

</details>


### [259] [Pose Anything Anywhere:Model-free Object Poses from Arbitrary References](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23634)
*Hongli Xu,Jiaqi Hu,Junwen Huang,Boyang Zhong,Peter KT Yu,Nassir Navab,Benjamin Busam,Slobodan Ilic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PANY是一个统一的、无需模型的6D姿态估计框架，支持RGB和RGB-D输入，可在少量无位姿参考视图上运行，通过多视图Transformer几何骨干网络学习跨视图一致几何和配准线索，在遮挡和大视角变化下显著优于现有方法（YCB-V +12%，LM-O +20%以上）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6D姿态估计方法中，基于模型的方法依赖CAD资产或繁重的上手流程；而大多数无模型方法仅限于成对单锚匹配，在遮挡和大视角变化下性能不佳。目标是提出一个统一的无模型框架，能在少量或无位姿参考视图下泛化到未见物体。

Method: 基于多视图Transformer几何骨干网络，超越成对匹配，学习视图一致的几何和跨视图对齐线索；当有额外无位姿辅助视图时，通过姿态图规范注册进行聚合以增加几何覆盖。

Result: 在多个基准上达到SOTA，YCB-V上姿态精度提升+12%，LM-O上提升超过+20%，在单参考和稀疏参考设置下均表现稳健。

Conclusion: PANY证明了无模型、多视图几何一致的框架可以有效替代依赖CAD的模型方法，在开放世界机器人6D姿态估计中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Estimating the 6D pose of unseen objects is a fundamental yet challenging problem for open-world robotics and embodied perception. Model-based methods are accurate but depend on CAD assets or heavy onboarding, while most model-free approaches are still limited to pairwise single-anchor matching and thus fail under occlusion and large viewpoint changes with low query-reference overlap. Therefore, we present PANY, a unified model-free framework that seamlessly supports both RGB and RGB-D inputs, operates on one or sparse pose-free reference views, and generalizes effectively to novel objects. Built on a multi-view transformer geometry backbone, PANY moves beyond pairwise matching by learning view-consistent geometry and cross-view alignment cues that remain stable under wide baselines and limited overlap. When additional unposed assist views are available, PANY aggregates them via pose-graph canonical registration to increase geometric coverage and reinforce the final pose. Extensive experiments show that PANY achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, substantially outperforming existing model-free methods, improving pose accuracy by +12% on YCB-V and over +20% on LM-O. Furthermore, PANY consistently performs well under both single-reference and sparse-reference settings, demonstrating strong robustness in real-world environments.

</details>


### [260] [Lightweight Neural Framework for Robust 3D Volume and Surface Estimation from Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23653)
*Diego E. Farchione,Ramzi Idoughi,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种全前馈框架，直接从多视图图像回归尺度归一化的体积和表面积及其不确定性，通过图解码器融合3D点云和2D特征，避免迭代优化，在低输入图像数量下仍优于现有方法，并在珊瑚监测、饮食分析和人体测量学中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有体积和表面积估计方法计算成本高，且在稀疏和噪声数据下性能差，缺乏高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 全前馈框架：从多视图图像直接回归尺度归一化的体积和表面积及其不确定性；通过图解码器融合3D点云重建与视图对齐的2D特征，无需迭代优化。

Result: 在低输入图像数量下优于SOTA方法，在珊瑚监测、饮食分析、人体测量学三个域中验证，在稀疏视图和资源受限场景下保持高性能。

Conclusion: 提供了一种高速、可扩展的精确几何估计替代方案，适用于稀疏视图和资源受限场景。

Abstract: Accurate volume and surface area estimation is critical for diverse applications, from marine ecology to medical diagnostics. However, existing methods often suffer from high computational costs and poor performance with sparse and noisy data. We propose a fully feed-forward framework that regresses scale-normalized volume and surface area and their associated uncertainties directly from multi-view images. By fusing 3D point cloud reconstructions with view-aligned 2D features through a graph-based decoder, our model bypasses iterative optimization, ensuring exceptional scalability and rapid inference. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art methods, particularly when operating with a low number of input images. Validated across coral monitoring, dietary analysis, and anthropometry, our proposed framework provides a robust, adaptable solution for quantitative shape analysis. This architecture provides a high-speed, scalable alternative for precise geometric estimation from visual data, maintaining high performance even in resource-constrained or sparse-view scenarios.

</details>


### [261] [GeoFidelity-Bench: Evaluating Segment-Level Geographic Fidelity in Text-to-Image Street-View Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23669)
*Kaizhen Tan,Hanzhe Hong,Siru Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoFidelity-Bench基准，用于评估文生图模型在街景生成中对特定道路片段的忠实度。基准包含7,117张来自109个OSM路段的图片，通过对比目标路段与同城最近路段、同城其他路段、异城路段的检索排名来测试局部判别能力。实验发现添加街道和社区名称可将Top-1检索准确率提升5.5个百分点，但目标与同城最近路段的相似度边际接近零，表明模型学到的是区域级合理性而非精确路段身份。GPS坐标作为文本附加无额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型能生成视觉合理的城市街景，但无法判断生成结果是否真正对应于特定的道路片段（而非城市先验）。需要系统评估模型的地理忠实度（geographic fidelity），即生成图像是否忠实于特定路段而非仅仅城市级别的常识。

Method: 构建包含7,117张Mapillary图像的基准，覆盖6大洲25城市109个OSM命名路段。设计参考面板排名机制：将生成面板与目标路段、同城最近路段、同城其他路段、异城路段的真实图像对比，以检索排名衡量忠实度。评测六种开源文生图模型在三种提示条件（仅城市、城市+街道+社区、附加GPS坐标）下的表现。

Result: 添加街道和社区名称使Top-1检索准确率提升5.5个百分点（95% CI: 3.4–7.7）。但目标路段与同城最近路段的相似度边际接近零，说明模型仅提升区域级合理性而非精确路段识别。错误街道名称提示实验表明只有部分增益来自正确名称。GPS坐标无明显额外收益。真实图像查询成功恢复路段身份，验证基准包含可用的路段级信号。

Conclusion: 当前文生图模型在城市/社区级别的街景生成上表现合理，但对特定路段的精确忠实度仍存在显著差距。GeoFidelity-Bench揭示了"看起来像该城市"与"确实是该路段"之间的鸿沟，为评估和推动地理忠实生成提供了基准。

Abstract: Text-to-image models can generate visually plausible city streets, but whether their outputs correspond to a requested road segment rather than a generic city prior remains unclear. We introduce GeoFidelity-Bench, a reference-panel benchmark for segment-conditioned geographic fidelity in street-view generation. It contains 7,117 curated Mapillary images covering 109 named OpenStreetMap road segments in 25 cities across six continents. For each generated panel, the benchmark ranks the target reference panel against panels from the nearest segment in the same city, other segments in the same city, and segments from other cities, making local discrimination rather than absolute target similarity the primary test. We evaluate six open-weight text-to-image generators under city-only, street-and-neighborhood, and GPS-augmented prompts. Adding street and neighborhood names is associated with an increase of 5.5 percentage points in top-1 retrieval accuracy over city-only prompts, with a 95% confidence interval from 3.4 to 7.7 percentage points. However, the similarity margin between the target and the nearest segment in the same city remains near zero, indicating that local names improve broad local plausibility more than exact segment identity. Prompts that keep the city fixed but use incorrect street or neighborhood names further show that only part of the gain depends on the correct local names, while appending raw GPS coordinates as ordinary text yields no statistically clear additional benefit. Held-out real-image queries successfully recover segment identity, showing that the curated references contain usable segment-level signal. GeoFidelity-Bench thus reveals a persistent gap between city- or neighborhood-plausible street-view generation and faithful generation for a specific road segment.

</details>


### [262] [IMAGIN-4D: Image-Guided Controllable Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23675)
*Sai Kumar Dwivedi,Federica Bogo,Buğra Tekin,Chenhongyi Yang,Nadine Bertsch,Tomas Hodan,Michael J. Black,Dimitrios Tzionas,Shreyas Hampali*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出IMAGIN-4D，一种基于扩散的HOI生成方法，通过空间-时间分解的参考图像条件化来控制人物-物体交互，解决文本和路径点无法唯一指定交互细节的歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HOI生成方法使用文本、物体几何和稀疏路径点作为条件，但这些信号无法唯一指定交互细节（如抓取方式、接近方向、身体姿态、物体姿态、接触点和布局），存在严重歧义。作者提出用参考图像作为视觉规范来解决这一歧义。

Method: 1) 空间条件：从参考图像中提取监督式的交互状态token（身体姿态、物体姿态、身体-物体接触、空间关系）；2) 时间条件：逐帧查询图像块生成帧感知token，使不同帧关注同一图像的不同视觉线索；3) 角色感知条件：文本/路径点/交互状态token使用独立AdaLN流，帧感知视觉token与运动token交叉注意力；4) 构建合成运动-图像渲染管线以获取成对数据。

Result: 在FBM和BEHAVE数据集上，IMAGIN-4D在细粒度交互控制上优于单token和统一图像条件化基线，同时保持了路径点跟随能力和运动质量。

Conclusion: 通过空间-时间分解的图像条件化，可以显著提高HOI生成中交互细节的可控性，解决文本和轨迹条件不充分的问题。

Abstract: Generating human-object interactions (HOI) is central to character animation, robotics, AR/VR, and embodied AI. Recent HOI generation methods synthesize motion from text, object geometry, and sparse waypoints, controlling action semantics and object trajectories. However, these signals underspecify interaction: the same prompt and trajectory can produce different grasps, approach directions, body poses, object poses, contacts, and body-object layouts. We address this ambiguity with a reference image as a visual specification of the desired interaction snapshot. However, a single global image representation conflates distinct cues and conditions all frames on identical visual evidence. We therefore introduce IMAGIN-4D, a diffusion-based HOI generator that decomposes image conditioning spatio-temporally. For spatial conditioning, IMAGIN-4D extracts supervised interaction-state tokens for body pose, object pose, body-object contact, and spatial relationships at the depicted frame. For temporal conditioning, it computes frame-aware tokens by querying image patches per generated frame, allowing sequence segments to attend to different visual cues from the same image. To balance image, text, and waypoint cues, IMAGIN-4D uses role-aware conditioning: text, waypoints, and interaction-state tokens use separate AdaLN streams, while frame-aware visual tokens cross-attend with motion tokens. Since HOI motion datasets lack paired images, we build a synthetic motion-to-image rendering pipeline from FullBodyManipulation (FBM) and introduce an image-adherence metric to evaluate whether generated motions match the reference snapshot. Experiments on FBM and BEHAVE show that IMAGIN-4D improves fine-grained interaction control over single-token and uniformly image-conditioned baselines while preserving waypoint-following and motion quality. Code and models will be released at https://imagin4d.github.io.

</details>


### [263] [AIR: Adaptive Interleaved Reasoning with Code in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23678)
*Cong Han,Xiaohan Lan,Haibo Qiu,Yujie Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展强化学习训练，使多模态大模型具备自适应交织推理能力，专攻代码增强型数值计算任务，提出包含两阶段冷启动数据构建、数据过滤策略和基于分组约束奖励函数的自适应工具调用策略的三组件解决方案，在评估基准上平均提升6.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交织推理工作主要聚焦视觉感知任务中的工具使用，依赖预定义启发式规则，无法处理数值计算问题。本文旨在让MLLM通过强化学习在复杂数值计算任务中实现自适应交织推理。

Method: 提出三组件解决方案：(1) 两阶段冷启动数据构建流水线；(2) RL数据集的数据过滤策略；(3) 基于分组约束奖励函数的自适应工具调用策略，用于生成交织推理轨迹。

Result: 强化学习训练后，评估基准平均提升6.1个百分点，交织推理样本准确率提升9.9个百分点，工具调用成功率超过95%。

Conclusion: 强化学习训练结合分组约束奖励函数能有效增强MLLM在数值计算任务中的自适应交织推理能力，工具调用成功率极高。

Abstract: Following the paradigm shift initiated by OpenAI o3, interleaved reasoning with code to enhance multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has become a pivotal research frontier. The existing literature focuses primarily on tool-use within vision-perception tasks. However, such approaches typically rely on predefined heuristics for visual manipulation and are inherently incapable of addressing numerical computation problems due to their exclusive focus on visual operations. This paper empowers MLLMs with adaptive interleaved reasoning capabilities through extended reinforcement learning training on code-augmented complex numerical computation tasks. To this end, we propose a comprehensive three-component solution consisting of: a two-stage cold-start data construction pipeline, data filtering strategies for RL dataset curation, and an adaptive tool-invocation strategy leveraging a group-constrained reward function for interleaved reasoning trajectories. Extensive experiments demonstrate that after Reinforcement Learning training with the group-constrained reward function, performance improves by an average of 6.1 percentage points (pp) on evaluation benchmarks. Specifically, the accuracy for interleaved reasoning samples increases by 9.9 pp, and the overall success rate of tool-use exceeds 95%. Our data and code are available at: https://github.com/CongHan0808/AIR.git.

</details>


### [264] [Semantic Browsing: Controllable Diversity for Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23679)
*Sara Dorfman,Maya Vishnevsky,Omer Dahary,Or Patashnik,Daniel Cohen-Or*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于文本层语义控制的多样性生成方法，通过VLM智能体工作流在文本空间中进行结构性变异，实现可导航的结构化图像画廊（语义浏览），而非依赖随机噪声或表层变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I模型生成的样本多样性退化，倾向于坍缩为单一视觉解释；已有方法产生的多样性来自偶然变化而非有意义的设计选择，缺乏结构化和语义可控性。

Method: 利用T2I模型训练于详细描述文本的特性，将语义决策与像素生成解耦；在文本层面引入多样性，使用VLM结合智能体工作流对完整场景上下文进行结构化变异，生成沿可解释语义轴变化的多样化文本描述，再送入T2I模型生成图像。

Result: 方法能生成多样且可导航的设计空间，每个变异对应一个用户可理解的语义决策，实现语义浏览式的创意探索。

Conclusion: 在文本层面进行语义控制的多样性生成是可行的，智能体工作流可克服标准VLM的通用输出问题，实现结构化的创意探索体验。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image models excel in visual fidelity and prompt adherence. However, this strict adherence comes at the cost of diversity: generated samples tend to collapse into a single visual interpretation. Existing methods to improve diversity produce outputs driven by incidental variations rather than meaningful design choices. This motivates a new variant of the diversity task where structure is enforced on the generated samples. We introduce a method for controlled diversity that enables Semantic Browsing, where users can navigate structured image galleries and experience creative exploration through a systematic traversal of meaningful, interpretable axes of variation. Achieving this level of semantic control requires a deep understanding of the scene. We exploit the fact that recent text-to-image models are trained on elaborated captions, effectively decoupling semantic decision-making from pixel generation. This enables a paradigm shift: instead of relying on stochastic variation within the text-to-image model, we induce diversity directly at the text level. By leveraging rich textual representations, we allow a Vision Language Model (VLM) to operate on the full scene context. To overcome the generic outputs typical of standard VLMs, we employ an agentic workflow that explicitly enforces structured variation attuned to the original prompt. We demonstrate that our method produces diverse and navigable design spaces where every variation corresponds to a specific, user-understandable semantic decision.

</details>


### [265] [Keep The Essentials: Efficient Reference Conditioned Generation via Token Dropping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23682)
*Rishubh Parihar,Ayush Raina,R. Venkatesh Babu,Or Patashnik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Sparse Context方法，通过在推理时丢弃大量参考token（结合任务感知的token选择策略）实现参考扩散模型4倍（多参考）和2倍（单参考）加速，且不损害视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参考扩散模型计算成本高，且随参考图像数量严重扩展，而现有扩散模型加速研究主要关注纯文本驱动生成，对参考模型的效率问题缺乏探索。

Method: 1) 训练时采用随机token丢弃（不同比例）来增强模型对稀疏参考表示的鲁棒性；2) 推理时使用任务感知的token选择策略（而非随机丢弃），优先保留最信息丰富的区域，动态调整token预算。

Result: 多参考生成加速4倍，单参考生成加速2倍，在空间对齐编辑和主体驱动生成任务上均不损害视觉质量。

Conclusion: 参考扩散模型中的密集token网格存在大量冗余，通过稀疏化表示可以大幅提升推理效率，且训练策略使模型脱离特定token选择规则的束缚，灵活适配不同任务。

Abstract: Reference-based diffusion models enable highly controllable image generation by leveraging elements from input images to guide prompt-driven synthesis. However, these models are computationally expensive in runtime, and their cost scales severely with the number of input references. While the efficiency of diffusion models has been extensively studied in the context of prompt-driven generation, it remains largely under-explored in the realm of reference-based models. This setting presents unique challenges not addressed by methods focusing solely on generation. In particular, the wasteful representation of references as dense token grids offers significant opportunities for improvement. In this work, we present Sparse Context, a method for constructing sparse reference representations by retaining only a reduced subset of reference tokens. We observe that even without modifying the model, dropping a significant portion of reference tokens at inference time largely preserves its generation capabilities. To fully realize this potential, we fine-tune the model with random token dropping at varying ratios, encouraging robustness to partial reference representations. Crucially, this training strategy decouples the model from any specific token selection rule, allowing flexible control at inference time. At inference time, instead of random dropping, we apply task-aware token selection strategies that prioritize the most informative regions of the reference images, adapting the token budget to the input and task requirements. Extensive experiments show our method achieves a 4x increase in inference speed for multi-reference generation and an 2x for single reference generation. Importantly, this efficiency is achieved without compromising visual quality across both spatially-aligned editing and subject-driven generation.

</details>


### [266] [Lift4D: Harmonizing Single-View 3D Estimation for 4D Reconstruction In-the-Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23688)
*Yehonathan Litman,Xiaoxuan Ma,Manan Shah,Nicolas Ugrinovic,Kris Kitani,Fernando De la Torre,Shubham Tulsiani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Lift4D提出了一种测试时优化框架，通过因果潜在条件将单视图3D重建模型适配为时序一致的多帧预测，初始化可变形3D高斯泼溅表示，然后通过遮挡感知优化和视图条件扩散先验对视频进行匹配，显著提升了复杂野外场景下的大形变和严重遮挡的4D重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么直接从视觉输入预测4D表示但受限于训练数据稀缺，要么先初始化3D表示再基于视频变形优化但仅在初始阶段使用先验、之后完全依赖视频监督，两者都难以处理大形变和严重遮挡的野外场景。

Method: 1) 将单视图3D重建模型通过因果潜在条件改造为时序一致的逐帧预测，提供变形3D高斯泼溅的连贯初始化；2) 遮挡感知优化：恢复可见表面细节，同时利用视图条件扩散先验补全未观测区域。

Result: 在具有严重遮挡和非刚性运动的复杂野外序列上，Lift4D明显优于现有的4D重建方法。

Conclusion: 结合时序一致的先验初始化和扩散先验引导的遮挡感知优化，可以有效处理野外大形变和遮挡场景下的动态非刚性物体4D重建。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic non-rigid objects from monocular video requires integrating visual cues from direct observations with data-driven priors over geometry and appearance. Prior approaches either learn to directly predict 4D representations from visual input or initialize a 3D representation that is subsequently deformed and refined based on video evidence. However, the former are constrained by the scarcity of 4D training data, while the latter leverage priors only for the initial reconstruction and rely solely on video supervision thereafter; neither handles complex in-the-wild scenarios with large deformations and occlusions well. We present Lift4D, a test-time optimization framework that addresses both limitations. First, we adapt an existing single-view 3D reconstruction model to yield temporally consistent per-frame predictions via causal latent conditioning, providing a coherent initialization for a deformable 3D Gaussian Splatting representation. We then ``sculpt'' this representation to match the input video through an occlusion-aware optimization that faithfully recovers visible surface details while completing unobserved regions using a view-conditioned diffusion prior. We demonstrate that Lift4D clearly improves over prior 4D reconstruction methods, particularly on challenging in-the-wild sequences with severe occlusions and non-rigid motion.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [267] [Real-World Deployment of Massively Parallel Sampling-Based MPC for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20712)
*Magnus Dierking,Joao Carvalho,An Thai Le,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于JAX大规模并行化和MuJoCo MJX高保真模拟器的采样MPC框架，在Franka Research 3机器人上实现了Push-T操作任务的完整real-to-sim-to-real流程。MTP变体（结构化全局采样）在需要模式切换的任务中优于CEM、MPPI、PS等单峰基线。同时评估了在线域随机化，发现接触初始化参数可提供可解释的适应信号，而全局物理参数反馈过弱难以在典型重规划频率下可靠利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的采样MPC在接触丰富的机器人操作中多依赖简化动力学或局限于仿真，缺乏高保真模拟与真实硬件部署的结合，且对域随机化在实时MPC中的有效性缺乏系统分析。

Method: 利用JAX进行大规模并行化加速计算，结合MuJoCo MJX高保真模拟器构建MPC框架；提出MTP（结构化全局采样）变体处理多峰动作分布；在模拟和Franka Research 3真实机器人上评估Push-T任务；分析在线域随机化在MPC样本预算内的表现。

Result: MTP变体在模式切换任务中优于CEM、MPPI、PS等单峰基线；接触初始化参数提供可解释的适应信号；全局物理参数反馈过弱，在典型重规划频率下无法可靠利用。

Conclusion: 采样MPC在接触丰富操作中面临接触敏感性、紧凑计算预算和难以实时获取信息丰富的域随机化信号三大挑战；MTP结构化采样是处理模式切换的有效策略。

Abstract: Sampling-based Model Predictive Control (SMPC) is a promising strategy for contact-rich robotic manipulation, combining gradient-free optimization with massively parallel GPU simulation. Yet, most prior work relies on simplified dynamics or remains confined to simulation. We present an MPC framework that leverages JAX for large-scale parallelization and efficient computation, coupled with the high-fidelity MuJoCo MJX simulator, and deploy it on a Franka Research 3 executing the Push-T manipulation task through a complete real-to-sim-to-real pipeline. The MTP variant with structured global sampling outperforms unimodal baselines such as CEM, MPPI, and PS across tasks that require mode switching, both in simulation and on hardware. Furthermore, we evaluate online domain randomization within the MPC sample budget, showing that contact-initiation parameters yield interpretable adaptation signals, whereas global physics parameters provide feedback that is too weak for reliable exploitation at typical replanning frequencies. These findings highlight key challenges for sampling-based MPC in contact-rich manipulation-contact sensitivity, tight compute budgets, and the difficulty of obtaining informative domain-randomization signals in real time.

</details>


### [268] [Coupled Routing and Configuration Optimization for Multi-Viewpoint Robotic Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20739)
*Minh Nhat Vu,Khang Nguyen,Vu Trung Tran,Vien Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一框架，联合优化9-DoF机器人系统（7-DoF KUKA LBR iiwa + 2-DoF线性平台）的6-DoF检测视点访问顺序与每个视点的逆运动学配置，实现时间最优的无碰撞路径。通过封闭形式参数化自运动流形、双积分器旅行时间代理、随机密钥编码和CMA-ES优化，仅对选中的路由边进行直接配点轨迹优化，将轨迹求解复杂度从二次降至线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模块化流水线先固定每个视点的IK配置，再构建全对旅行时间图，最后求解路由，这种解耦方式无法实现全局时间最优。

Method: 1) 封闭形式参数化每个视点的3D自运动流形；2) 封闭形式的可容许双积分器代理近似旅行时间；3) 随机密钥编码访问顺序与配置；4) CMA-ES优化联合目标函数；5) 仅对选定路由边进行直接配点轨迹优化验证动态可行性与力矩约束。

Result: 仿真与真实KUKA LBR iiwa + 2-DoF线性平台实验验证了可行性、平滑执行和减少的端到端检测时间，优于模块化和基于距离的基线方法。

Conclusion: 联合优化视点顺序与IK配置可显著缩短检测时间，且将轨迹求解复杂度从二次降为线性，避免模块化流水线的次优解耦。

Abstract: We present a unified framework that turns a set of 6-DoF inspection viewpoints into a time-optimal, collision-free route for a 9-DoF robotic system. Unlike modular pipelines that fix a single inverse-kinematics (IK) configuration per viewpoint, build an all-pairs travel-time map, and then route, our method jointly optimizes the visiting order and the per-viewpoint configuration in a single global search. The three-dimensional self-motion manifold of each viewpoint is parameterized in closed form so that the pose constraint holds by construction, the rest-to-rest travel time is approximated by a closed-form admissible double-integrator surrogate, and the tour is encoded by random keys. A derivative-free optimizer (CMA-ES) minimizes a cheap penalized objective over order and configuration, after which direct-collocation trajectory optimization is applied only to the edges of the selected route to certify dynamic feasibility and torque limits, and to return exact timings. This reduces the trajectory solves from quadratic to linear in the number of viewpoints and removes the decoupling that prevents modular pipelines from being globally time-optimal. Simulations and real-robot experiments on a KUKA LBR iiwa with a 2-DoF linear stage validate feasibility, smooth execution, and reduced end-to-end inspection time relative to modular and naive distance-based baselines.

</details>


### [269] [A Digital Twin Framework for Traffic-Aware UAV Pavement Monitoring without Lane Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20742)
*Yamil Uchani,Grace Abigail Luna Verdueta,Mauricio Figueroa,Edwin Salcedo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于Unity的数字孪生框架，用于交通感知的无人机路面巡检。采用YOLOv8n检测+分类的两阶段方法，在模拟器上达99.26%准确率，并评估了三种遮挡恢复策略在不同交通密度和飞行高度下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机路面巡检在实际部署中受交通、行人和临时遮挡影响导致缺陷可见性差，需要一种无需封闭车道的交通感知巡检策略评估方法。

Method: 构建Unity数字孪生环境，集成程序化生成的缺陷、动态交通和行人、自主无人机导航及嵌入式损伤感知pipeline。使用两阶段方法：YOLOv8n检测器定位缺陷/行人/车辆，第二级分类器区分坑洞/单裂缝/鳄鱼裂缝。评估三种恢复策略：悬停重检、微重定位、跳过回访。

Result: 模拟器测试集五类总体准确率99.26%。悬停重检在中高交通下覆盖率最高达97.03%，跳过回访在低交通下最优达97.95%。飞行高度对覆盖范围影响显著。

Conclusion: 数字孪生可以支持交通感知无人机巡检策略的开发与评估，在实际部署前提供有效验证平台。

Abstract: UAV-based pavement inspection can reduce the cost and risk of road-surface monitoring, but real-world deployment remains difficult when traffic, pedestrians, and temporary occlusions affect the visibility of defects. This paper presents a Unity-based digital twin framework for traffic-aware UAV pavement monitoring without lane closure. The proposed environment integrates procedurally generated road defects, dynamic vehicles and pedestrians, autonomous UAV navigation, and an embedded road-damage perception pipeline. The perception module uses a two-stage approach: a lightweight YOLOv8n detector first localises road defects, pedestrians, and vehicles, while a second classifier distinguishes among potholes, single cracks, and crocodile cracks. On the simulator test set, the full pipeline achieved 99.26% overall accuracy across five classes. The digital twin was then used to evaluate three recovery strategies for occluded road segments: hover-and-recheck, micro-repositioning, and skip-and-revisit. Experiments were conducted across different traffic densities and flight altitudes using coverage, mission time, energy consumption, and revisit ratio as operational metrics. Results show that flight altitude has a strong influence on inspection coverage and that adaptive recovery improves performance under occlusion. In particular, hover-and-recheck achieved the most consistent coverage under medium and high traffic conditions, reaching up to 97.03% coverage, while skip-and-revisit was most effective in low-traffic scenarios, reaching 97.95\% coverage at medium altitude. These results demonstrate that digital twins can support the development and evaluation of traffic-aware UAV inspection strategies before real-world deployment.

</details>


### [270] [Toward Machine Risk Perception: Integrating Trust Calibration and Precursor-Based Risk Estimation for Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20748)
*He Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于前兆信号和信任校准的人形机器人主动风险感知框架，采用Logistic-Exponential耦合模型对事故演化进行序贯建模，并在模拟案例中验证了早期干预的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于力/距离阈值的反应式安全范式无法捕捉人形机器人动态、类人运动失败的序贯性和不确定性，亟需一种能根据事故前兆信号进行主动风险感知的框架。

Method: 构建Logistic-Exponential (LE)耦合模型，将来自多种前兆信号的逻辑斯蒂增长与时间衰减相结合；将信任定义为估计事故概率的倒数，使机器人实时调整行为激进程度；基于126个事件和241个前兆的多源数据集识别12种主导事故模式。

Result: 识别出12种主导事故模式，大多数前兆在1秒内重叠出现；"fall-onto-human"模拟案例验证了LE-Trust耦合可触发早期干预并防止倒塌。

Conclusion: 将人形机器人安全从静态阈值推进到动态、基于证据的推理，为工业5.0中人机协作建立了风险感知和可信赖的基础。

Abstract: Humanoid robots are emerging as co-workers in smart manufacturing, yet their dynamic, human-like movements introduce safety risks that differ fundamentally from those of fixed or wheeled robots. Conventional safety paradigms based on reactive force or distance limits fail to capture the sequential, uncertain nature of humanoid failures. This study proposes a precursor-driven, trust-calibrated framework to enable proactive humanoid risk perception. Accident evolution is modeled through sequential precursor cues using a Logistic-Exponential (LE) formulation that couples logistic escalation from diverse precursors with exponential decay for temporal dissipation. Trust is defined as the inverse of the estimated accident probability, allowing humanoids to adapt behavior in real time, reducing aggressiveness when risk intensifies, and restoring confidence as stability returns. A multi-source dataset of 126 documented events and 241 precursors revealed twelve dominant accident modes, most evolving through overlapping cues within one second. A simulated case study ("fall-onto-human") demonstrated how the LE-Trust coupling can trigger early intervention and prevent collapse. The results advance humanoid safety from static thresholds toward dynamic, evidence-based inference, establishing a foundation for risk-aware and trustworthy human-robot collaboration in Industry 5.0 environments.

</details>


### [271] [Perturbation-Based Uncertainty for Failure Detection in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20754)
*Yousung Lee,Dongsoo Har*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于隐藏激活扰动的标签无关、模型无关的推理时不确定性估计框架，通过向Transformer隐藏激活注入高斯扰动并评估扰动后动作预测的差异性来量化不确定性，在LIBERO和LIBERO-PRO上优于基于采样的不确定性方法，有效提升分布漂移下的故障检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代VLA模型（如基于回归或流生成）无法提供显式预测概率，现有不确定性方法依赖随机动作采样或监督故障标签，泛化性受限，亟需一种适用于多种预训练VLA模型的标签无关、模型无关的不确定性量化方法。

Method: 在推理时向Transformer隐藏激活注入高斯扰动，从扰动后的动作预测差异中估计认知不确定性（epistemic signals），方法受贝叶斯视角下局部模型变化的启发，无需标签、无需修改模型结构。

Result: 在LIBERO和LIBERO-PRO数据集上，基于扰动的不确定性在分布漂移下的故障检测性能一致优于基于采样的不确定性方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架能为VLA模型提供实用的不确定性信号，不依赖特定模型架构或监督标签，具有良好的通用性和实际部署潜力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong performance in robotic manipulation, but reliable uncertainty quantification remains challenging, particularly under distribution shift. Unlike autoregressive policies, many modern VLA models generate continuous actions through regression or flow-based generation, where explicit predictive probabilities are unavailable. Moreover, existing approaches often rely on stochastic action sampling or supervised failure labels, limiting their applicability across diverse pretrained VLA models. In this work, we propose a label-free and model-agnostic framework for inference-time uncertainty estimation through hidden activation perturbations, motivated by Bayesian perspectives on local model variations. Specifically, we inject Gaussian perturbations into transformer hidden activations and estimate epistemic signals from disagreement across perturbed action predictions. Experiments on LIBERO and LIBERO-PRO show that perturbation-based uncertainty consistently improves failure detection under distribution shift compared to sampling-based uncertainty, providing a practical uncertainty signal for VLA models.

</details>


### [272] [UNSEEN: Uncertainty-aware Navigation via Sparse Estimation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20755)
*Tommaso Faraci,Marco Camurri,Daniele Fontanelli,Luigi Palopoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UNSEEN提出了一种统一的、感知不确定性的视觉导航框架，仅使用前向摄像头，通过耦合定位、建图和规划，在资源受限平台上实现鲁棒导航，在真实实验中ATE降低9.8%，估计精度提升45%，任务成功率100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉导航方法要么依赖多模态传感器增加成本，要么是松耦合模块化流水线，忽略运动与感知之间的紧耦合，且在运动模糊、低纹理、光照变化下性能严重退化。感知感知方法通常只优化单个模块，无法在整个导航栈中一致传播不确定性。

Method: 提出UNSEEN框架，以6Hz频率估计稀疏地图和机器人位姿及其不确定性，并利用这些信息在滚动时域中联合优化任务进度和估计精度来规划轨迹，仅使用前向单目摄像头。

Result: UNSEEN-SLAM绝对平移误差降低9.8%，UNSEEN-Plan估计精度提升最高45%，任务成功率100%。仿真和大量真实未知环境实验验证了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 紧密耦合不确定性感知的视觉导航框架在资源受限平台上显著优于现有方法，验证了统一不确定性传播对鲁棒导航的有效性。

Abstract: Visual navigation in unknown environments remains a core challenge in mobile robotics, especially for resource-constrained platforms. Most existing approaches rely on loosely coupled modular pipelines and strong assumptions on perception quality or environmental structure, often resorting to multi-modal sensor suites that increase system complexity and deployment cost. Vision-only navigation offers a lightweight alternative, but its performance degrades severely under motion blur, low texture, and illumination changes, largely because they neglect the tight coupling between commanded motion and perception. While perception-aware methods partially address this issue, they typically optimize individual modules and fail to propagate uncertainty consistently across the navigation stack. In this paper, we present UNSEEN, a unified uncertainty- and perception-aware navigation framework that explicitly couples localization, mapping, and planning using only a front-mounted camera. UNSEEN estimates sparse maps and robot poses with associated uncertainties at 6Hz, and leverages them to plan trajectories that jointly optimize task progress and estimation accuracy in receding-horizon. Simulations and extensive real-world experiments in unknown environments demonstrate the robustness of the proposed approach, with UNSEEN-SLAM reducing absolute translational error by 9.8% and UNSEEN-Plan improving estimation accuracy by up to 45% compared to state-of-the-art methods, while achieving a 100% task success rate.

</details>


### [273] [Mind the Privileged-to-Camera Gap: Actor-Centric Sidecar Supervision for Camera-First Open-Loop Waypoint Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20772)
*Feeza Khan Khanzada,Jaerock Kwon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究自动驾驶开放环路的监督表示学习，通过在训练时引入模拟器生成的旁路标签（sidecar labels）来监督道路参与者的表征学习（actor grounding、相关性、短时运动），在不增加推理输入的情况下，将终点位移误差（FDE）从1.815m降至1.223m（降幅32.6%），但证据仅限开环仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的以摄像头为主的自驾模型仅通过未来航点（waypoint）监督训练，缺乏对附近道路参与者（如车辆、行人）显式的actor级表示监督，导致模型对动态环境的理解不够充分。作者希望通过监督表示学习来弥补这一差距。

Method: 在训练时，利用模拟器生成的sidecar标签提供三类额外监督：actor grounding（检测道路参与者）、priviledged hindsight actor relevance（评估哪些actor与历史轨迹相关）、selected-actor short-horizon motion（预测选定参与者的短时运动）。推理时仅使用多视图RGB、自车状态和路径指令，不增加推理负担。对比基线包括纯航点RGB baseline和无sidecar的RGB控制组。

Result: RU-sidecar方法在开环仿真中将FDE从1.815±0.02m降低至1.223±0.01m（降幅32.6%），在1494条路线中有1445条改善。对含有至少4个有效actor的样本改善29.1%，对存在弱势道路使用者（VRU）的样本改善30.0%。含教师对齐的版本达到1.186±0.15m，但种子方差更大。

Conclusion: 通过侧监督信号隐式提升actor级表示可以显著改善航点预测精度，说明显式监督道路上其他参与者的表征对自驾规划有益。但结果仅限于开环仿真诊断，无法保证闭环驾驶性能提升，且存在特邀者到摄像头的性能差距（privileged-to-camera gap）。

Abstract: Camera-first autonomous-driving models predict future ego waypoints from images, ego-state features, and route commands, but waypoint supervision alone does not explicitly supervise actor-level representations of nearby road users. We study this as supervised representation learning for open-loop waypoint prediction. The deployable model uses multi-view RGB, ego state, and route command at inference. During training, simulator-derived sidecar labels supervise actor grounding, privileged hindsight actor relevance relative to the logged ego trajectory, and selected-actor short-horizon motion; these labels are never inference inputs. We evaluate route-disjoint splits with matched architecture, optimizer, validation criterion, checkpoint selection, and three seeds. A plain waypoint-only RGB baseline obtains 1.815$\pm$0.02 m final displacement error (FDE), and the matched no-teacher non-sidecar RGB control obtains 1.716$\pm$0.02 m. Road-user sidecar supervision (RU-sidecar) reduces FDE to 1.223$\pm$0.01 m, a 32.6% reduction over the plain baseline and 28.7% over the matched no-teacher non-sidecar RGB control. It improves over the plain baseline on 1445/1494 routes and over the matched no-teacher non-sidecar RGB control on 1417/1494 routes. Actor-conditioned slices show gains in all nonempty subsets, including 29.1% reduction for samples with at least four valid sidecar actors and 30.0% when a vulnerable road user is present. Optional simulator-state teacher alignment reaches 1.186$\pm$0.15 m FDE, but higher seed variability makes it secondary. Non-deployable simulator-state diagnostics remain stronger, indicating a privileged-to-camera gap. The evidence is limited to open-loop simulation diagnostics.

</details>


### [274] [World Action Models: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20781)
*Qiuhong Shen,Shihua Zhang,Yue Liao,Qi Li,Zhenxiong Tan,Shizun Wang,Shuicheng Yan,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理了世界行动模型（WAMs），澄清了其与视频生成模型、VLA策略等概念的边界，并提出了两种互补的分类视角：按预测输出类型（渲染未来、隐式未来、无需视频生成的动作推理）和按技术组件分解（预测基底、骨干网络、动作耦合方式、部署模式）。文章总结了交互性、因果性、持续性、物理合理性、泛化性等关键维度，指出WAMs的设计趋势是生成更少的未来但保留控制所需的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界行动模型（WAMs）领域发展迅速，既有基于视频生成模型的方法，也有基于语言/视觉-语言骨干的方法，导致"世界模型"、"视频生成模型"、"VLA策略"等概念边界模糊，亟需系统性梳理和统一认知框架。

Method: 采用两种互补视角组织现有工作：1) 按预测输出类型分类（渲染未来、隐式未来、无视频生成的动作推理）；2) 按技术组件分解（预测基底、骨干网络、动作耦合方式、部署模式）。基于此统一框架讨论交互性、因果性、持续性、物理合理性、泛化性等关键属性。

Result: 揭示了WAMs的一致设计模式：WAMs不是简单的"视频生成器+动作头"，而是预测-动作方法，其设计选择在表征丰富度与计算、内存、延迟、动作标签成本之间做权衡。领域趋势是向"生成更少未来但保留控制所需"的方向发展。

Conclusion: WAMs的核心在于权衡表征丰富度与资源消耗，未来方向是生成更精简但更具控制价值的预测，同时指出数据、评估和开放性挑战仍需攻克。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) are embodied predictive-action models that make a forecast of the future available to action. Recent WAMs repurpose large video generation models, and a parallel line relies on language or vision-language backbones without a video-generation core. This rapid expansion has blurred the boundary among broad world models, video generation models, action-grounded video world models, Vision-Language-Action policies, and WAMs. This survey gives the field a common account. It first clarifies these boundaries, then organizes existing works through two complementary views. The first view asks what each method is required to generate, spanning rendered futures, latent futures, and video-generation-free action reasoning. The second view decomposes each method by predictive substrate, backbone, action coupling, and deployment regime. This anatomy supports a unified discussion of interactability, causality, persistence, physical plausibility, and generalization, followed by data, evaluation, and open challenges. Across these axes, a consistent design pattern emerges: WAMs are not simply video generators with action heads, but predictive-action methods whose design choices trade representational richness against compute, memory, latency, and action-label cost. The field is moving toward methods that generate less of the future while preserving what control requires. The survey homepage is available at https://world-action-models.github.io/.

</details>


### [275] [Geometric Entropy: When Trajectory Diversity Helps and Hurts in Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20871)
*Qian Luo,Ruizhe Liu,Pei Zhou,Xunzhe Zhou,Yanchao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出几何熵（H_G）作为衡量演示轨迹几何多样性的任务无关指标，发现IL成功率与H_G呈倒U型关系：低多样性提升鲁棒性，过高多样性引起策略歧义；且最优熵随数据量增加、任务变简单或更强先验而向低值偏移。H_G可用于数据集快速预审和校准演示数据到可学习区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习研究缺乏对演示轨迹形状多样性的系统量化分析，不清楚何种多样性程度对IL学习最有利，需要一种任务无关的指标来度量和指导演示数据集的构建。

Method: 提出几何熵（H_G），通过对轨迹进行目标帧对齐以消除位姿和工作空间尺度等外在变化，仅保留内在几何多样性。在多种IL架构（含VLA）、模拟和真实机器人接触式操作任务上进行系统实验验证。

Result: 发现IL成功率与H_G呈倒U型关系；最优熵随数据增多/任务变易/更强先验而降低；对预训练VLA模型呈单调递减趋势。H_G可高效预审数据集并校准演示数据。

Conclusion: 几何熵是有效的任务无关演示多样性度量指标，提供了校准演示数据集到可学习区的实用指南，对VLA等大规模预训练模型的数据筛选有直接指导意义。

Abstract: We study how trajectory-shape diversity in demonstrations affects imitation learning (IL) performance across models, tasks, and data scales. We introduce Geometric Entropy (H_G), a task-agnostic metric that quantifies the intrinsic diversity of transit trajectories after normalizing away extrinsic variation, such as goal pose and workspace scale, via target-frame alignment. Across multiple IL architectures and both simulated and real-robot contact-rich manipulation tasks, we observe a consistent inverted-U relationship between success and H_G: increasing geometric diversity improves robustness in low-diversity regimes but degrades performance once diversity induces strategy ambiguity. Moreover, the optimal entropy shifts toward lower values as task mastery increases through more data, easier tasks, or stronger priors, and for a pretrained vision-language-action model the trend becomes effectively monotonic decreasing. Practically, H_G enables fast pre-training auditing of demonstration datasets and offers a simple guideline for calibrating demonstrations toward the learnable regime.

</details>


### [276] [Vesta: A Generalist Embodied Reasoning Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20905)
*Johan Bjorck,Zhiqi Li,Yunze Man,Jing Wang,An-Chieh Cheng,Sifei Liu,Shihao Wang,Zhiding Yu,Abhishek Badki,Stan Birchfield,Valts Blukis,Yevgen Chebotar,Siyi Chen,Sicong Leng,Yu-Cheng Chou,Tianli Ding,Boyi Li,Zhengyi Luo,Hang Su,Jonathan Tremblay,Tingwu Wang,Bowen Wen,Jimmy Wu,Xianghui Xie,Hanrong Ye,Hongxu Yin,K. R. Zentner,Liangyan Gui,Yu-Xiong Wang,Yuke Zhu,Linxi "Jim" Fan,Jan Kautz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Vesta 是一个统一的具身通用模型，将定位、空间推理、导航和长程规划整合到一个基础模型中，使用大规模空间诱导语料库和简单的多模态记忆机制，在多个基准上平均优于专用 SOTA 模型 >20%，优于最佳集成 >10%，在真实机器人任务上成功率提升 >35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统通常堆叠多个专用模型（定位、导航、规划等），计算开销大、易出现级联错误，缺乏统一且高效的解决方案。

Method: 1) 构建大规模多样化语料库以诱导空间基础能力；2) 设计简单的多模态记忆机制支持长时程推理；3) 将所有能力（定位、空间推理、导航、长程规划）统一到一个单一基础模型中。

Result: 在多个基准上平均优于专用 SOTA 模型 >20%，优于每个类别最佳模型的集成 >10%；在需要记忆和推理的真实机器人任务上成功率提升 >35%。

Conclusion: 证明单一通用模型在具身智能中是可行、可扩展且优于组合专用模型的可选方案。

Abstract: Robots operating in open-world environments must seamlessly integrate localization, spatial reasoning, navigation, and long-horizon planning. While specialist models excel at individual tasks, deploying a multi-model stack is computationally expensive and prone to cascading errors. We present Vesta, a unified embodied generalist that consolidates these capabilities into a single foundation model. Our approach combines a diverse and massive curated corpus designed to induce spatial grounding and a simple multimodal memory harness that enables reasoning over extended time horizons. Across diverse benchmarks, Vesta on average beats individual SOTA baselines by >$20\%$ and beats an ensemble of per-category-best baselines by $>10\%$ -- thus demonstrating that a generalist model can match or exceed specialists. On real-world robotic tasks requiring memory and reasoning, Vesta improves task success by >35\%. Our work thus demonstrates that a single generalist is a feasible, scalable, and arguably preferable alternative to combining specialists.

</details>


### [277] [Learning-Based Modeling of Soft Robots via Cosserat Rod Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20958)
*Mohammad Ali,Nithin Senthur Kumar,Eric J. Barth,Thomas Beckers*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种端口-哈密顿高斯过程回归框架，融合Cosserat杆理论与哈密顿物理，实现平面杆状软体机器人的精确且能量一致的动力学学习与仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人动力学建模面临挑战：基于第一性原理的模型耗时长且表达能力有限，数据驱动模型缺乏可解释性和物理一致性。

Method: 将Cosserat杆理论与端口-哈密顿框架结合，利用高斯过程回归进行数据驱动推断，在保留系统能量结构的同时学习杆动力学。

Result: 数值仿真表明该方法能够实现杆状软体机器人的准确且能量一致的动力学表示。

Conclusion: 该方法为复杂连续介质力学建模提供了一条鲁棒且可解释的途径。

Abstract: Modeling soft robot dynamics is challenging due to their continuum structure and typically nonlinear dynamics. Creating models based on first-order principles is typically time-demanding, and their expressiveness is limited, whereas data-driven models lack interpretability and physical consistency. This work aims to overcome these challenges by introducing a port-Hamiltonian Gaussian Process Regression framework for learning and simulating the dynamics of planar, rod-like soft robots. In detail, the proposed model integrates Cosserat rod theory and Hamiltonian physics with data-driven inference to preserve the system's energy structure while accurately learning the rod dynamics. Numerical simulations show that we can achieve accurate and energy-consistent representations of a rod-like soft robot, showing the potential for a robust and interpretable pathway for modeling complex continuum mechanics.

</details>


### [278] [Heterogeneous Policy Networks for Composite Robot Team Communication and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20962)
*Esmaeil Seraj,Rohan Paleja,Luis Pimentel,Kin Man Lee,Zheyuan Wang,Daniel Martin,Matthew Sklar,John Zhang,Zahi Kakish,Matthew Gombolay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出异构策略网络(HetNet)，通过异构图注意力网络实现异构多智能体团队的高效通信与协调，支持二值化消息传递，在多个领域实现5.84%-707.65%性能提升并降低200倍通信带宽。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MARL方法使用同构图网络无法有效建模异构智能体（不同状态、动作、观察空间）的通信模式，导致通信低效甚至损害团队性能。

Method: 基于异构图注意力网络扩展HetNet，学习异构协作策略并支持端到端训练的二值化消息传递，实现高效通信。

Result: 相比次优基线实现5.84%-707.65%性能提升，同时通信带宽降低200倍。

Conclusion: HetNet在异构多智能体团队的协调与通信学习上达到了新SOTA，兼具高性能与极高通信效率。

Abstract: High-performing human-human teams learn intelligent and efficient communication and coordination strategies to maximize their joint utility. These teams implicitly understand the different roles of heterogeneous team members and adapt their communication protocols accordingly. Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) has attempted to develop computational methods for synthesizing such joint coordination-communication strategies, but emulating heterogeneous communication patterns across agents with different state, action, and observation spaces has remained a challenge. Without properly modeling agent heterogeneity, as in prior MARL work that leverages homogeneous graph networks, communication becomes less helpful and can even deteriorate the team's performance. In the past, we proposed Heterogeneous Policy Networks (HetNet) to learn efficient and diverse communication models for coordinating cooperative heterogeneous teams. In this extended work, we extend Heterogeneous Policy Networks (HetNet) to support scaling heterogeneous robot teams. Building on heterogeneous graph-attention networks, we show that HetNet not only facilitates learning heterogeneous collaborative policies but also enables end-to-end training for learning highly efficient binarized messaging. Our empirical evaluation shows that HetNet sets a new state of the art in learning coordination and communication strategies for heterogeneous multi-agent teams by achieving an 5.84% to 707.65% performance improvement over the next-best baseline across multiple domains while simultaneously achieving a 200x reduction in the required communication bandwidth.

</details>


### [279] [Duet: Dual-Robot Understanding via Efficient Teaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20990)
*Yiqi Zhao,Ruohai Ge,Celina Shiyu Wang,Junjie Ye,Muchen Xu,Minhao Li,Sergey Zakharov,Basile Van Hoorick,Vitor Campagnolo Guizilini,Leonidas Guibas,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Jyotirmoy V. Deshmukh,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DUET是一个面向双机器人移动操作的学习框架，通过VR遥操作和人类协作演示采集，结合Action Chunking Transformer架构预训练+微调，实现高效的双机器人协作策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双机器人协作可超越单机器人能力限制，但数据采集是训练这类系统的主要瓶颈。现有方法缺乏高效的跨机器人平台数据采集和利用人类协作先验的方法。

Method: 1) 构建统一的双体VR遥操作系统用于异构机器人数据采集；2) 开发人体协作追踪管线记录人类协作移动操作先验；3) 基于Action Chunking Transformer的架构，先在高效的人类协作演示上预训练，再在少量真实机器人遥操作轨迹上微调。

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人和Dexmate Vega1移动操作手上验证了四个协作任务，利用人类先验的方法在任务性能上优于纯机器人数据训练的基线，且遥操作数据采集速度平均提升5.4倍，在所有任务上性能不低于纯机器人数据策略。

Conclusion: 利用人类协作先验不仅能提升任务性能，还能显著减少数据采集所需的人力成本，验证了人机协作知识迁移的有效性。

Abstract: Dual-robot collaboration enables tasks that exceed the reach and payload of a single robot, such as collaboratively transporting objects across environments and executing coordinated handovers. Data acquisition is the primary bottleneck for training these systems. To this end, we introduce DUET, a dual-robot learning framework for mobile manipulation. For efficient data collection, we create a unified dual-embodiment synchronized VR-based teleoperation system for in-domain heterogeneous robot data collection. We further develop a complementary tracking pipeline that records human-human coordination and collaborative mobile manipulation priors. To allow efficient learning, we introduce an Action Chunking Transformer based architecture that first pretrains collaborative policies on efficient human-human demonstrations, before finetuning them on a minimal set of real-robot teleoperation trajectories. We develop a benchmark of four collaborative tasks to evaluate our framework using a Unitree G1 humanoid and a Dexmate Vega1 mobile manipulator. The results demonstrate that harnessing human priors not only yields superior task performance compared to baselines trained only on robot data, but also reduces the total human effort required for data collection. Our human data collection pipeline achieves 5.4x acceleration on average from teleoperation, but we perform equally or better than robot-only data trained policies across all tasks. Our project page is available at https://zhaoy37.github.io/Duet/.

</details>


### [280] [Inductive Generalization for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20999)
*Annabella Macaluso,Haochen Zhang,Ishaan Masilamony,Yingshan Chang,Yonatan Bisk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"归纳泛化"(Inductive Generalization)评估协议，用于测试视觉运动策略在逐步变难的分布外任务变体上的推理能力。实验表明现有SOTA策略（包括VLA模型）虽然能应对光照、杂乱等非结构化域偏移，却在归纳泛化测试中失败，揭示了与数据和模型扩展正交的关键学习挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉运动策略的泛化评估主要依赖已知分布上的非结构化域偏移（如光照、杂乱），无法真正衡量策略在逐步变难的分布外任务变体上的归纳推理能力。作者借鉴语言模型中的轴基评估方法，旨在建立更能反映真实泛化能力的评估协议。

Method: 提出可重用的形式化评估协议，通过设计难度递增的分布外任务变体来测试策略的归纳泛化能力。在多种现有范式（包括SoTA VLA模型）上建立基线，评估其在归纳泛化测试中的表现。

Result: 现有策略（包括Vision-Language-Action模型）在应对先前的域偏移（如干扰物等）时表现出泛化能力，但在归纳泛化测试中全部失败。揭示了与数据和模型扩展正交的一类学习挑战。

Conclusion: 归纳泛化是实现通用机器人不可或缺的能力，现有范式和扩展策略无法解决这一问题，需要新的学习范式来弥补这类能力缺口。

Abstract: Understanding the generalization capabilities of visuomotor policies is essential in the development of capable robotic agents. Generalizable models learn structures that transfer across domains. However, in practice, visuomotor policies test performance by interpolation on known distributions using unstructured domain shifts (e.g. lighting, clutter, diverse objects). We argue that to measure generalization capabilities we must instead test the inductive capacity of policies on progressively harder, out-of-distribution task variants. We call this inductive generalization, drawing directly on how axis-based evaluation has revealed inherent generalization limitations in language models (e.g. sequence length, counting) arXiv:2502.00197 . We provide a reusable and formal evaluation protocol for measuring inductive generalization in any manipulation policy, and establish baselines showing that existing paradigms fail this test; e.g. SoTA Vision-Language-Action models and find that policies that appear to generalize to prior domain shifts (distractors, etc) fail inductive generalization tests. These results expose a class of learning challenges orthogonal to those addressed by data and model scaling in robot learning, yet are imperative to solve in order to realize general purpose robots.

</details>


### [281] [R2HandoverSim: A Simulation Framework and Benchmark for Robot-to-Human Object Handovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21011)
*Hanxin Zhang,Abdulqader Dhafer,Hongbiao Dong,Zhou Daniel Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: R2HandoverSim是一个用于机器人向人类递送物品（R2H handover）的仿真基准测试平台，包含标准化评估协议、四种基线方法对比、30人用户研究，以及五个互补指标（规划可行性、可达性、抓取稳定性、抓取可用性、安全性），证明仿真结果与真实世界评估相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人向人类递送物品的方法进展迅速，但缺乏标准化评估协议，导致无法客观比较不同方法。

Method: 构建仿真基准测试平台，系统比较四种基线方法的共享抓取位姿预测，进行30人用户研究，提出五个互补评估指标。

Result: 仿真结果与真实世界评估结果相关，五个互补指标比单一成功率更能反映用户感知的递送质量。

Conclusion: R2HandoverSim为R2H手递物提供了标准化、可复现的评估基准，多指标评估体系更有效。

Abstract: We present R2HandoverSim, a simulation benchmark for robot-to-human (R2H) object handovers. Although R2H handover methods have advanced rapidly, the lack of standardized evaluation protocols impedes objective comparison. Our benchmark enables reproducible evaluation by systematically comparing four baselines on their predicted shared grasp poses. We conduct a user study with 30 participants, analyze baseline performance, and show that simulation results correlate with real-world evaluation outcomes. Crucially, five complementary metrics (planning feasibility, reachability, grasp stability, grasp affordance, and safety) better reflect user-perceived handover quality than overall success rate alone. Website and code: https://robot-future.github.io/r2handoversim/.

</details>


### [282] [BayesFP: Posterior Estimation for Flow-Based Policies via Feynman-Kac Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21014)
*Sreevardhan Sirigiri,Weiming Zhi,Fabio Ramos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Feynman-Kac校正器的推理时后验采样方法，无需重新训练即可为预训练的扩散策略和流匹配策略生成满足安全约束和任务目标的轨迹，统一了扩散策略和流策略的约束轨迹生成框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人轨迹生成需要在保持专家行为保真度的同时满足推理时才指定的安全约束和任务目标，现有方法往往需要重新训练或仅适用于特定策略类型，缺乏统一的推理时约束采样框架。

Method: 将约束轨迹生成建模为贝叶斯后验采样（以专家演示分布为先验，以推理时代价函数导出的似然进行倾斜），利用Feynman-Kac校正器框架从后验中采样，并将其从扩散模型扩展到确定性流匹配策略，无需重新训练。

Result: 在Diffusion Policy、GR00T-N1.6和π₀.₅预训练检查点上验证，涵盖仿真和真实世界的操作任务（包括推理时引入的非凸障碍物规划），并在零样本任务上展示了相比基础π₀.₅的改进。

Conclusion: 该方法为扩散和流策略提供了统一的推理时、无需重新训练的约束轨迹生成方案，有效提升了预训练策略在推理时约束下的性能。

Abstract: Robots must generate trajectories that remain faithful to learned expert behavior while satisfying safety constraints and task-specific objectives specified only at inference time. We formulate constrained trajectory generation for pretrained diffusion and flow-matching policies as Bayesian posterior sampling, with the learned demonstration distribution as a prior and an inference-time, cost-derived likelihood tilting it toward feasible, optimal trajectories. To sample from this posterior without any retraining of the base policy, we leverage the Feynman--Kac corrector framework, originally formulated for diffusion models, and extend it to deterministic flow-matching policies. The result is a unified, inference-time, retraining-free sampler for diffusion and flow policies. We validate the approach on pretrained Diffusion Policy, GR00T-N1.6, and $π_{0.5}$ checkpoints across simulated and real-world manipulation tasks, including planning around non-convex obstacles introduced at inference time, and show improvements over the base $π_{0.5}$ on zero-shot tasks.

</details>


### [283] [Membrane-based Acoustic Microrobots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21047)
*Fatih Kocabas,Cemal Polat Avdar,Prithvi Venkatesh,Yunus Alapan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于柔性PDMS膜封装的声学微型机器人，通过物理隔离微腔内的空气气泡防止气体溶解，解决了传统声学微机器人因共振频率漂移导致操作寿命短的问题，实现了超过24小时的连续稳定推进与微流控，并结合磁性微粒实现了低强度磁场定向控制，且可缩放到~100微米尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声学微型机器人依赖微腔内捕获的空气微泡产生声学驱动，但气泡会随时间溶解导致共振频率偏移，显著限制了机器人的操作寿命和可靠性，迫切需要一种能长期稳定运行的声学微执行器方案。

Method: 在微腔上方键合一层柔性PDMS薄膜作为物理屏障防止气体扩散，同时保留声学微流控能力；嵌入磁性微粒实现磁场定向控制；系统表征了连续24小时以上的驱动稳定性，并展示了100微米尺度的可扩展性。

Result: 基于PDMS膜的声学执行器在连续驱动24小时后仍保持稳定的流场和推进性能；在2 mT低强度磁场下实现定向控制；设计架构可缩放到~100微米。

Conclusion: 膜基封装策略为开发高耐久性声学微型执行器和微型机器人提供了可靠的框架，有望支持长期体内外任务。

Abstract: Acoustic microrobots have emerged as a promising frontier for targeted drug delivery and minimally invasive medicine due to their high-power density and biocompatibility. Despite wide-ranging designs, conventional acoustic microrobots mostly rely on air microbubbles trapped within confined microcavities within the robot body, which suffer from limited operational longevity due to rapid gas dissolution and resultant shifts in resonance frequency. In this paper, we propose a robust, membrane-based acoustic microrobot that overcomes these limitations by employing a thin flexible Polydimethylsiloxane (PDMS) membrane bonded over confined microcavities for microstreaming. The introduced design physically prevents gas diffusion, ensuring stable performance over extended periods at high actuation voltages. We systematically characterized the membrane-based acoustic actuator longevity, demonstrating consistent streaming and propulsion for over 24 hours of continuous operation. In addition, by embedding magnetic microparticles into the structural body, these actuators were successfully employed as microswimmers with directional control using low-intensity (2 mT) external magnetic fields. Finally, we demonstrate the scalability of the proposed design architecture down to ~100 um. This membrane-based approach establishes a reliable framework for the development of high-endurance acoustic microactuators and microrobots capable of performing long-term tasks.

</details>


### [284] [ReFPO: Reflow Regularization for Flow Matching Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21086)
*Ge Wang,Yibo Peng,Fan Feng,Shenhao Yan,Chengsi Yao,Jiahao Yang,Honghao Cai,Yiming Zhao,Xi Li,Jinke Ren,Shuguang Cui,Yatong Han,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReFPO通过在FPO中引入显式Reflow正则化（仅需一行代码修改），实现了高效稳定的流匹配策略梯度强化学习，支持高保真单步推理，在GridWorld、MuJoCo和Humanoid任务上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Flow Matching Policy Gradients (FPO)存在训练不稳定、CFM代理比率尖峰等问题，且多步推理效率低。作者发现FPO梯度更新可解释为隐式优势加权Reflow过程，因此引入显式几何正则化来提升稳定性和单步推理质量。

Method: ReFPO将显式Reflow正则化项集成到FPO的PPO-style训练中，仅需一行代码修改。该方法结合优势引导更新与路径修正(path rectification)，减少CFM代理比率尖峰，稳定训练，无需额外计算开销或辅助蒸馏阶段。

Result: 在GridWorld、MuJoCo Playground和高维Humanoid Control任务中，ReFPO提升了平均性能和离散化鲁棒性，单步推理质量常匹配或超越多步推理性能，训练更稳定。

Conclusion: ReFPO为基于流的生成策略提供了一种可扩展、稳定的在线RL方法，在复杂物理仿真中展现良好效果，且实现简单。

Abstract: We present Reflow-regularized Flow Matching Policy Gradients (ReFPO), a simple online RL method that adds explicit Reflow regularization to FPO for efficient flow-based control. We uncover a key structural property: the gradient updates in Flow Matching Policy Gradients (FPO) can be interpreted as an implicit advantage-weighted Reflow process, providing a new geometric perspective on flow-based policy gradients. Building on this insight, ReFPO introduces an explicit geometric regularizer that can be implemented with a single line of code change without incurring additional computational overhead or auxiliary distillation stages. By synergizing advantage-guided updates with path rectification, our method reduces CFM proxy-ratio spikes, stabilizes PPO-style training, and enables high-fidelity one-step inference that often matches or exceeds multi-step performance. We experimentally demonstrate that ReFPO improves average performance and discretization robustness across GridWorld, MuJoCo Playground, and high-dimensional Humanoid Control tasks, providing a scalable and stable approach for generative policies in complex physical simulations.

</details>


### [285] [MV-WAM: Manifold-Aware World Action Model with Value Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21088)
*Jintao Chen,Peidong Jia,Qingpo Wuwu,Jiaming Liu,Mengfei Du,Chun-Kai Fan,Xiaowei Chi,Hao Chen,Chengyu Bai,Zezhong Qian,Hao Wang,Jiajun Cao,Weishi Mi,Xiaozhu Ju,Jian Tang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MV-WAM框架，通过跨模态因果掩码、流形感知优化和进度-价值调节机制，解决视觉与动作模态异质性导致的泛化问题。在RoboTwin仿真和双机械臂真实任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型在分布外场景下泛化能力不足，归因于视觉与动作模态的流形结构不匹配导致联合优化损害动作鲁棒性。

Method: 提出MV-WAM端到端框架：1) 跨模态因果掩码实现视觉预测、动作生成和价值估计的层次化对齐；2) 流形感知优化显式处理模态异质性；3) 进度-价值调节机制通过检测预测帧与动作偏移实现自主纠错。

Result: RoboTwin仿真随机场景55.7%成功率，超越基线29.3%；真实双机械臂四个任务平均77.5%成功率。

Conclusion: 流形感知的跨模态对齐是实现鲁棒策略泛化的关键，为可部署机器人操作提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Achieving robust and generalizable manipulation across diverse environments remains a fundamental challenge in embodied robotics. Recent world action models achieve strong in-domain performance, yet their gains do not extend proportionally to out-of-distribution scenarios. We attribute this to a structural mismatch between visual and action modalities, whose intrinsically heterogeneous manifolds cause joint optimization to disproportionately degrade action robustness under distribution shift. To address this, we propose MV-WAM, a novel end-to-end framework that jointly models visual prediction, action generation, and value estimation designed to effectively leverage video priors during both training and inference for enhanced action generalization. Key to this unification is a cross-modality causal mask that hierarchically grounds actions in predicted video frames and value function tokens in both modalities. To further narrow the generalization gap, MV-WAM adopts a manifold-aware optimization scheme that explicitly accounts for the structural heterogeneity across modalities. Finally, MV-WAM introduces a progress-value regulation mechanism that estimates task completion and detects misalignment between predicted frames and generated actions, enabling the policy to autonomously identify execution deviations and recover through value-guided rollback. On the RoboTwin simulation, MV-WAM achieves a 55.7% mean success rate on random scenarios without any randomized action supervision, outperforming the strongest baseline by 29.3%. MV-WAM achieves a 77.5% mean success rate across four real-world tasks of varying difficulty on a dual-arm robot. Our results demonstrate that manifold-aware cross-modal alignment is essential for robust policy generalization, offering a path toward deployable robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [286] [How Should a Robot Configure Its Laser Scanner for Inspection?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21093)
*Zhiling Chen,David Gorsich,Matthew P. Castanier,Yang Zhang,Jiong Tang,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SenseHD提出一种将扫描仪配置建模为指令条件感知决策的机器人检测系统，通过超维联想记忆将感知参数离散化为感知动作并选择稳定感知域，在真实机器人平台上显著提升检测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人检测中传感参数配置对测量质量有决定性影响，但现有工作主要关注机器人运动与视点规划，传感参数配置问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将传感参数配置建模为指令条件感知决策，把连续参数离散化为感知动作，通过超维联想记忆选择稳定感知域，避免精确参数值预测。

Result: 在真实机器人检测平台上，SenseHD能够鲁棒地选择合适传感配置，显著提升检测可靠性，且相比基线方法更轻量高效。

Conclusion: SenseHD验证了将传感参数配置作为离散感知动作选择问题的有效性，为机器人检测中的感知配置自动化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Robotic inspection relies on accurate sensing to acquire high-fidelity geometric measurements for defect detection and metrology. While prior work has focused on robot motion and viewpoint planning, how to configure sensing parameters remains largely underexplored, despite their decisive impact on measurement quality. We propose SenseHD, a robotic sensing system that formulates scanner configuration as an instruction-conditioned sensing decision. Instead of predicting precise parameter values, SenseHD treats sensing parameters as discrete sensing actions and selects stable sensing regimes through hyperdimensional associative memory. Experiments on a real robotic inspection platform demonstrate that SenseHD robustly selects appropriate sensing configurations and significantly improves inspection reliability, while remaining lightweight and efficient compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [287] [Factor-Aware Mixture-of-Experts with Pretrained Encoder for Combinatorial Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21100)
*Feihong Zhang,Guojian Zhan,Zeyu He,Yinuo Wang,Likun Wang,Tianze Zhu,Yao Lyu,Tao Zhang,Tinghao Yi,Wei You,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FAME是一个集成因子感知混合专家与预训练编码器的框架，通过三阶段训练（策略预热、因子特定适配器训练、联合微调）提升扩散策略在视觉机器人操作中对光照、表面纹理等环境变化的泛化能力，在Meta-World基准上提升34%，在真实拾取放置任务中提升35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练编码器+扩散策略在视觉机器人操作中虽为主流范式，但在复杂环境（光照、表面纹理等变化）下泛化能力不足。

Method: 三阶段训练：(1) 策略预热——在标准环境数据上训练扩散策略，冻结编码器；(2) 因子特定适配器训练——在冻结编码器与策略之间插入轻量适配器，每个适配器针对一种环境变化训练；(3) 联合微调——中央router与预热后的策略在混合数据上训练，router作为密集MoE加权冻结的因子特定适配器，实现组合泛化。

Result: Meta-World基准上比扩散策略基线提升34%；真实世界拾取放置任务中提升35%。

Conclusion: 因子感知的MoE框架能有效提升扩散策略在多种环境变化下的泛化能力，且训练高效（仅训练轻量适配器+router+策略）。

Abstract: The integration of pretrained encoders with diffusion policies has become a dominant paradigm for visual robotic manipulation. However, it still struggles to generalize across complex environments with varying factors such as lighting and surface textures. To address this, we propose FAME, a framework that integrates a factor-aware mixture-of-experts (MoE) with a pretrained encoder to enhance generalization to environmental variations. FAME follows a three-stage training process: (1) policy warmup, where a diffusion policy is trained on standard-environment data with a frozen encoder; (2) factor-specific adapter training, where lightweight adapters inserted between the frozen encoder and the temporarily frozen policy are trained on customized datasets, each targeting a distinct environmental variation; and (3) joint fine-tuning, where a central router and the warmed policy are trained on mixed data to handle multiple factors jointly. FAME is ``factor-aware'' because the central router softly weights frozen factor-specific adapters as a dense MoE, enabling combinatorial generalization across multiple factors. Evaluations on the Meta-World benchmark show that FAME outperforms diffusion policy baselines by 34%. We further validate FAME in a real-world pick-and-place task using a compact model trained on newly collected data, where FAME achieves a 35% improvement in generalization under real-world variations.

</details>


### [288] [PoLAR: Factorizing Extent and Mode in Latent Actions for Robot Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21139)
*Youngjoon Jeong,Jihwan Yu,Minsoo Jo,Junha Chun,Taesup Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PoLAR是一种在潜在动作空间上施加径向-方向结构的方法，通过双曲空间建模，使半径编码转变程度、方向编码转变模式，利用时间偏移作为转变程度的弱代理，在模拟和真实机器人实验中提升下游策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在动作预训练方法将每个转移编码为单一非结构化表示，混淆了转移程度和转移模式，缺乏归纳偏置来区分二者。

Method: 在潜在动作空间上施加径向-方向结构：半径编码转变程度（利用时间偏移作为弱监督），方向保留转变模式；将该结构实例化在双曲空间中，利用其半径增大时体积扩张的特性自然适配更多样化的转变模式。

Result: 在任务内和大规模预训练设置中，PoLAR在模拟和真实机器人实验中均优于潜在动作基线和强预训练VLA基线，提升下游策略性能。

Conclusion: 潜在动作空间的几何结构是视觉预训练迁移到下游机器人策略学习的重要设计选择。

Abstract: Latent action pretraining learns representations of visual change from pairs of observations, but existing methods typically encode each transition as a single unstructured representation that entangles transition extent and transition mode. We introduce Polar Latent Actions with Radial structure (PoLAR), which imposes a radial-direction structure on latent actions, encouraging radius to encode transition extent and direction to retain transition mode. PoLAR uses temporal offset between two observations as a weak proxy for transition extent, encouraging latent action from observation pairs separated by larger temporal gaps to occupy larger radii. We instantiate this structure in hyperbolic space, whose expanding volume with radius offers a natural fit for more diverse transition modes at larger extents. Across in-task and large-scale pretraining settings, PoLAR improves downstream policy performance in simulation and real-world robot experiments, outperforming latent action baselines and strong pretrained VLAs. These results suggest that the geometry of the latent action space is an important design choice for transferring visual pretraining to downstream robot policy learning.

</details>


### [289] [Pose-Agnostic Robotic Functional Grasping via Observation-Action Canonicalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21148)
*Le Qiu,Cole Harrison,Jiankai Sun,Yao Liu,Suning Huang,Qianzhong Chen,Yang You,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyMug是一个规范化视觉运动强化学习框架，通过观测-动作规范化将深度观测和预测末端执行器动作转换到共享的物体中心坐标系，使得单一闭环策略在仿真中训练后能零样本迁移到真实机器人上实现杯柄抓取，在仿真中达到93%+成功率，真实Franka Panda机器人上达到80%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能性机器人抓取需要策略能泛化到不同物体几何和姿态，同时保持任务特定的接触精度。现有方法中，抓取姿态检测是开环的且对薄柄结构的估计误差敏感；学习的视觉运动策略隐式处理不同物体放置带来的视觉外观和动作方向耦合变化，限制了泛化能力。

Method: 提出AnyMug框架：(1) 观测-动作规范化(Observation-Action Canonicalization)，将深度观测和预测的动作变换到物体中心坐标系；(2) 手柄感知奖励函数(Handle-Aware Reward)，鼓励精确接近、夹爪对齐和对置手指放置；(3) 姿态课程学习(Pose Curriculum)和域随机化(Domain Randomization)提升训练稳定性和仿真到现实的迁移。

Result: 仿真中：未见过的直立和倒置杯子均达到93%+成功率。真实Franka Panda机器人：零样本迁移，5个真实杯子在两种姿态类别上达到80%成功率。

Conclusion: AnyMug通过规范化框架有效解耦了物体姿态变化与策略学习，实现了从仿真到真实机器人零样本迁移的功能性抓取，验证了规范化方法在解决耦合的视觉外观和动作方向变化问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Functional robotic grasping requires a policy that generalizes across diverse object geometries and poses while maintaining task-specific contact precision. We study this challenge through mug-handle grasping, where thin handles, instance variation, and upright or inverted placements make both perception and control sensitive to object configuration. Grasp pose detection methods operate open-loop and are sensitive to estimation errors on thin handle structures. Learned visuomotor policies must implicitly learn to handle the coupled variation in visual appearance and action direction induced by different object placements, limiting generalization. We propose AnyMug, a canonicalized visuomotor reinforcement learning framework for functional grasping that trains a single closed-loop policy entirely in simulation and deploys it zero-shot on a real robot. AnyMug introduces observation-action canonicalization, which transforms both the depth observation and the predicted end-effector action into a shared object-centric frame. The policy therefore sees a consistent mug-centered view and emits actions in a canonical direction regardless of mug placement, allowing the same grasping behavior to be reused across configurations. A handle-aware reward further encourages precise approach, gripper alignment, and opposing-finger placement, while a pose curriculum and domain randomization improve training stability and sim-to-real transfer. In simulation, AnyMug achieves over 93% success rate on both unseen upright and inverted mugs and transfers zero-shot to a real Franka Panda, reaching 80% success rate on 5 held-out physical mugs across both pose categories.

</details>


### [290] [OmniV2X: A Generative Foundation Planner for Efficient End-to-End Cooperative Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21165)
*Juntong Peng,Juanwu Lu,Yupeng Zhou,Can Cui,Yaobin Chen,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniV2X是一个用于车联网(V2X)协同驾驶的生成式基础模型，通过多模态多智能体上下文序列直接进行轨迹规划，在DAIR-V2X-Seq数据集上以不到10%的微调数据和不到1%的通信带宽实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有V2X协同驾驶方法存在三个关键问题：密集3D感知的高计算成本、协同场景数据稀缺导致脆弱性、以及多模态融合时与标准化消息协议的兼容性差。需要一种更高效、数据效率更高且符合标准的基础模型。

Method: 提出OmniV2X生成式基础模型，直接处理多模态多智能体独立上下文序列；采用端到端监督训练，通过下游轨迹生成损失进行训练；使用高容量生成序列规划器通过交叉注意力注入隐式利用多模态输入；在单智能体规划数据集上预训练后，通过轻量级标准兼容V2X token高效适配协同环境。

Result: 在DAIR-V2X-Seq数据集上，OmniV2X超越现有端到端协同驾驶基线，使用不到10%的微调V2X数据集和不到1%的通信带宽达到SOTA性能；全面评估证明了其在真实约束下的计算效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: OmniV2X证明了生成式基础模型范式在V2X协同驾驶中的有效性，具有极高的数据效率和通信效率，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present OmniV2X, a generative foundation model for vehicle-to-everything (V2X) cooperative driving. The model directly interprets independent context sequences comprising multi-modal and multi-agent observations. The new design mitigates the computational cost of dense 3D perception, the vulnerability to data scarcity in cooperative scenarios, and the poor compliance with standardized messaging in existing methods that fuse multi-modal inputs into a shared representation. For training, we present an end-to-end supervised pipeline using a downstream trajectory generation loss, in which a high-capacity generative sequence planner implicitly learns to steer the model and leverage multi-modal inputs via cross-attention injection. As a foundation model, we demonstrate that OmniV2X pre-trained on large-scale single-agent planning datasets can efficiently adapt to cooperative environments by integrating the conditioning context with lightweight, standard-compliant V2X tokens.
